                {item.aiAnalysis?.tags && item.aiAnalysis.tags.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-gray-700/50">
                        {item.aiAnalysis.tags.map((tag, idx) => (
                            <span key={idx} className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-gray-700 text-gray-400 text-[10px] rounded-full">
                                #{tag}
                            </span>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                )}

            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

// --- COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL ---
export const InteractionTimeline: React.FC<InteractionTimelineProps> = ({ clientId }) => {
    
    const { interactions, isLoading, error, refreshTimeline } = useClientTimeline(clientId);

    if (isLoading) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-gray-500 space-y-3">
                <FaSync className="animate-spin w-6 h-6 text-accent-secondary" />
                <span className="text-xs">Carregando histórico...</span>
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (error) {
        return (
            <div className="p-4 text-center text-xs text-red-400 bg-red-900/10 rounded-lg">
                <p>{error}</p>
                <button onClick={() => refreshTimeline()} className="mt-2 underline hover:text-white">Tentar novamente</button>
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (interactions.length === 0) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-gray-600 opacity-60">
                <FaClock className="w-10 h-10 mb-2" />
                <p className="text-sm">Nenhuma interação registrada.</p>
            </div>
        );
    }

    return (
        <div className="relative pl-4 space-y-6 py-2 animate-fade-in custom-scrollbar pr-2">
            
            {/* Linha Vertical Conectora */}
            <div className="absolute left-2 top-4 bottom-4 w-0.5 bg-gray-700 pointer-events-none"></div>

            {interactions.map((item) => (
                <TimelineItem key={item.id} item={item} />
            ))}
            
            {/* Marcador de Fim */}
            <div className="relative pl-6 pt-2 opacity-50">
                <div className="absolute left-[3px] top-3 w-2 h-2 bg-gray-700 rounded-full"></div>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">Início do relacionamento</span>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/features/crm/components/client-widget/ClientListItem.tsx =====

// src/features/crm/components/client-widget/ClientListItem.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { FaBuilding, FaEnvelope, FaEdit, FaTrash, FaUndo } from 'react-icons/fa'; // Novos ícones
import { Client } from '@/core/types/client';

interface ClientListItemProps {
    client: Client;
    onClick: (client: Client) => void;
    isSelected?: boolean;
    // --- NOVAS PROPS DE AÇÃO ---
    onEdit?: (client: Client) => void;
    onDelete?: (client: Client) => void;
}

export const ClientListItem: React.FC<ClientListItemProps> = ({ 
    client, 
    onClick, 
    isSelected = false,
    onEdit,
    onDelete
}) => {
    
    const baseClasses = "p-3 rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition border group relative"; // Adicionado relative
    const stateClasses = isSelected 
        ? "bg-gray-800 border-accent-secondary shadow-md" 
        : "border-transparent hover:bg-gray-700 hover:border-gray-600";

    // Função auxiliar para evitar propagação do clique (selecionar o card)
    const handleAction = (e: React.MouseEvent, action?: (c: Client) => void) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (action) action(client);
    };

    return (
        <div 
            onClick={() => onClick(client)}
            className={`${baseClasses} ${stateClasses}`}
        >
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                
                {/* Lado Esquerdo: Dados */}
                <div className="flex-1 pr-2">
                    <h3 className={`text-sm font-bold ${isSelected ? 'text-white' : 'text-gray-200 group-hover:text-white'}`}>
                        {client.name}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 font-mono mt-0.5">
                        {client.document || 'Lead (Sem Doc)'}
                    </p>
                    
                    {/* Informação Secundária */}
                    <div className="mt-2 flex items-center text-xs text-gray-400">
                        {client.email ? (
                            <>
                                <FaEnvelope className="mr-1.5 w-3 h-3 text-gray-600 group-hover:text-accent-primary transition-colors" />
                                <span className="truncate max-w-[140px]">{client.email}</span>
                            </>
                        ) : (
                            <>
                                <FaBuilding className="mr-1.5 w-3 h-3 text-gray-600" />
                                <span className="italic opacity-50">Sem contato</span>
                            </>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                {/* Lado Direito: Status e Ações */}
                <div className="flex flex-col items-end space-y-2">
                    
                    {/* Badge de Status */}
                    <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border ${
                        client.isActive 
                            ? 'bg-green-900/20 text-green-400 border-green-900/30' 
                            : 'bg-red-900/20 text-red-400 border-red-900/30'
                    }`}>
                        {client.isActive ? 'Ativo' : 'Inativo'}
                    </span>

                    {/* --- BOTÕES DE AÇÃO (Só aparecem no Hover ou se Selecionado) --- */}
                    <div className={`flex space-x-2 ${isSelected ? 'opacity-100' : 'opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100'} transition-opacity duration-200`}>
                        
                        {/* Botão Editar */}
                        <button 
                            onClick={(e) => handleAction(e, onEdit)}
                            className="p-1.5 text-gray-400 hover:text-accent-secondary hover:bg-gray-600 rounded transition"
                            title="Editar Cliente"
                        >
                            <FaEdit className="w-3 h-3" />
                        </button>

                        {/* Botão Excluir/Ativar */}
                        <button 
                            onClick={(e) => handleAction(e, onDelete)}
                            className={`p-1.5 rounded transition ${client.isActive 
                                ? 'text-gray-400 hover:text-danger hover:bg-gray-600' 
                                : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-success hover:bg-gray-600'}`}
                            title={client.isActive ? "Desativar (Lixeira)" : "Reativar"}
                        >
                            {client.isActive ? <FaTrash className="w-3 h-3" /> : <FaUndo className="w-3 h-3" />}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/features/crm/components/client-widget/ClientListWidget.tsx =====

// src/features/crm/components/client-widget/ClientListWidget.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FaUser, FaPlus, FaExclamationTriangle } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useClients } from '@/core/hooks/useClients';
import { Client } from '@/core/types/client';
import { ClientModal } from '@/core/components/client/ClientModal';
import { ClientSearchInput } from './ClientSearchInput';
import { ClientListItem } from './ClientListItem';
// NOVO IMPORT: Hook de Mutação para o Delete
import { useClientMutation } from '@/core/hooks/useClientMutation';

interface ClientListWidgetProps {
    onSelectClient?: (client: Client | null) => void;
    refreshTrigger?: number;
    onNewClient: () => void;
    onEditClient: (client: Client) => void;
}

export const ClientListWidget: React.FC<ClientListWidgetProps> = ({ 
    onSelectClient,
    refreshTrigger = 0,
    onNewClient,
    onEditClient
}) => {
    
    const { 
        clients, 
        isLoading, 
        error, 
        searchTerm, 
        setSearchTerm, 
        refreshData 
    } = useClients(refreshTrigger);

    // Hook de Mutação para ações destrutivas/edição
    const { deleteClient, updateClient, isLoading: isMutating } = useClientMutation();

    const [selectedClientId, setSelectedClientId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    
    
    // --- HANDLERS ---

    /**
     * @method handleSelect
     * @description Gerencia a seleção e "deseleção" (Toggle) de um cliente.
     * @update Agora verifica se o ID já está selecionado para limpar o estado (Toggle).
     */
    const handleSelect = (client: Client) => {
        // Verifica se o cliente clicado JÁ É o que estava selecionado
        if (selectedClientId === client.id) {
            // CENÁRIO: Desmarcar (Limpar Seleção)
            setSelectedClientId(null);
            if (onSelectClient) {
                onSelectClient(null); // Avisa ao pai que não tem mais contexto
            }
        } else {
            // CENÁRIO: Marcar Novo
            setSelectedClientId(client.id);
            if (onSelectClient) {
                onSelectClient(client);
            }
        }
    };

    const handleSuccessCreateOrUpdate = () => {
        refreshData(); // Atualiza a lista
    };

    const handleNewClient = () => {
        onNewClient();
    };

    const handleEdit = (client: Client) => {
        onEditClient(client);
    };

/**
     * @method handleDelete
     * @description Agora atua como um Toggle Inteligente (Ativar/Desativar).
     */
    const handleDelete = async (client: Client) => {
        const isActive = client.isActive;
        const actionText = isActive ? 'mover para a lixeira' : 'reativar';
        const confirmText = `Tem certeza que deseja ${actionText} o cliente "${client.name}"?`;

        if (confirm(confirmText)) {
            try {
                if (isActive) {
                    // Se está ativo, desativa (Soft Delete)
                    await deleteClient(client.id);
                } else {
                    // Se está inativo, reativa (Update simples)
                    await updateClient(client.id, { isActive: true });
                }
                // Atualiza a lista visualmente
                refreshData(); 
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Falha ao alterar status:", error);
                alert(`Erro ao ${actionText} cliente.`);
            }
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="col-span-3 bg-base-metallic rounded-xl border border-gray-700 flex flex-col overflow-hidden shadow-lg h-full">
            
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-800 flex justify-between items-center flex-shrink-0">
                <h2 className="font-bold text-white flex items-center">
                    <FaUser className="mr-2 text-accent-secondary" /> Clientes
                </h2>
                <button 
                    onClick={handleNewClient}
                    className="text-xs bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-600 text-white px-2 py-1 rounded flex items-center transition shadow-sm"
                    title="Adicionar Novo Cliente"
                >
                    <FaPlus className="mr-1" /> Novo
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* Input de Busca */}
            <div className="flex-shrink-0">
                <ClientSearchInput 
                    value={searchTerm} 
                    onChange={setSearchTerm} 
                    isLoading={isLoading || isMutating} 
                />
            </div>

            {/* Lista */}
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-2 space-y-2 relative">
                
                {error && (
                    <div className="p-4 text-center text-xs text-red-400 bg-red-900/10 rounded-lg border border-red-900/30">
                        <FaExclamationTriangle className="mx-auto mb-2 w-5 h-5 opacity-50" />
                        <p>{error}</p>
                        <button onClick={() => refreshData()} className="mt-2 text-accent-primary hover:underline">
                            Tentar novamente
                        </button>
                    </div>
                )}

                {!isLoading && !error && clients.length === 0 && (
                    <div className="text-center py-10 text-gray-500 text-sm">
                        <p>Nenhum cliente encontrado.</p>
                        {searchTerm && <p className="text-xs mt-1 opacity-70">Tente outro termo de busca.</p>}
                    </div>
                )}

                {clients.map(client => (
                    <ClientListItem
                        key={client.id}
                        client={client}
                        onClick={handleSelect}
                        isSelected={selectedClientId === client.id}
                        // NOVAS PROPS CONECTADAS
                        onEdit={handleEdit}
                        onDelete={handleDelete}
                    />
                ))}
            </div>

            <div className="p-2 border-t border-gray-700 bg-gray-800/50 text-[10px] text-gray-500 text-center flex-shrink-0">
                {clients.length} registro(s) carregado(s)
            </div>

        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/features/crm/components/client-widget/ClientSearchInput.tsx =====

// src/features/crm/components/client-widget/ClientSearchInput.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { FaSearch } from 'react-icons/fa';

interface ClientSearchInputProps {
    value: string;
    onChange: (newValue: string) => void;
    isLoading?: boolean;
}

/**
 * @component ClientSearchInput
 * @description Campo de busca visual com ícone e estado de loading opcional.
 * * Pure Component: Não possui lógica de negócio, apenas UI.
 */
export const ClientSearchInput: React.FC<ClientSearchInputProps> = ({ 
    value, 
    onChange,
    isLoading = false
}) => {
    return (
        <div className="p-3 border-b border-gray-700 bg-base-metallic">
            <div className="relative">
                {/* Ícone: Alterna entre Lupa e Spinner se estiver carregando */}
                <div className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-500 w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center">
                    {isLoading ? (
                        <span className="animate-spin h-3 w-3 border-2 border-accent-secondary border-t-transparent rounded-full" />
                    ) : (
                        <FaSearch />
                    )}
                </div>
                
                <input 
                    type="text" 
                    value={value}
                    onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Buscar por nome, CPF ou CNPJ..." 
                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 text-sm text-white pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent-secondary focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent-secondary transition-all placeholder-gray-500"
                />
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/features/crm/components/communication/WhatsAppWidget.tsx =====

// src/features/crm/components/communication/WhatsAppWidget.tsx

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaWhatsapp, FaQrcode, FaUnlink, FaCheckCircle, FaSync, FaExclamationTriangle } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useMessaging } from '@/core/hooks/useMessaging';

/**
 * @component WhatsAppWidget
 * @description Gerencia a conexão com a instância do WhatsApp (Evolution API).
 * Exibe QR Code para pareamento ou Status de Conectado.
 */
export const WhatsAppWidget: React.FC = () => {
    
    const { 
        connectionStatus, 
        isLoading, 
        error, 
        checkStatus, 
        connectSession, 
        disconnectSession 
    } = useMessaging();

    // Verifica o status ao montar o componente
    useEffect(() => {
        checkStatus();
    }, [checkStatus]);

    // --- HANDLERS ---
    
    const handleConnect = () => {
        connectSession();
    };

    const handleDisconnect = () => {
        if (confirm("Tem certeza que deseja desconectar o WhatsApp deste Tenant?")) {
            disconnectSession();
        }
    };

    // --- RENDERIZAÇÃO DE ESTADOS ---

    // 1. Loading
    if (isLoading) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-gray-400 space-y-3 p-6">
                <FaSync className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-accent-primary" />
                <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">Comunicando com o Servidor...</span>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // 2. Erro
    if (error) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-red-400 space-y-3 p-6 text-center">
                <FaExclamationTriangle className="w-8 h-8" />
                <span className="text-xs font-bold">{error}</span>
                <button 
                    onClick={() => checkStatus()} 
                    className="text-xs underline hover:text-white"
                >
                    Tentar Novamente
                </button>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // 3. Conectado (Sucesso)
    if (connectionStatus?.isConnected) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full space-y-4 p-6 bg-green-900/10 rounded-lg border border-green-900/30">
                <div className="relative">
                    <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-green-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-green-900/50">
                        <FaWhatsapp className="w-8 h-8 text-white" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-5 h-5 bg-green-400 border-2 border-base-metallic rounded-full"></div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="text-center">
                    <h3 className="text-white font-bold text-lg">WhatsApp Conectado</h3>
                    <p className="text-green-400 text-xs uppercase tracking-wide mt-1">
                        Instância: {connectionStatus.instanceName}
                    </p>
                </div>

                <button 
                    onClick={handleDisconnect}
                    className="flex items-center px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 hover:bg-red-900/50 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition border border-gray-700 hover:border-red-800"
                >
                    <FaUnlink className="mr-2" /> Desconectar
                </button>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // 4. Desconectado (Com ou Sem QR Code)
    return (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full space-y-6 p-6">
            
            {/* Se tiver QR Code, mostra ele. Senão, mostra o ícone genérico */}
            {connectionStatus?.qrCode ? (
                <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-lg shadow-xl animate-fade-in">
                    {/* Renderiza a string Base64 como imagem */}
                    <img 
                        src={connectionStatus.qrCode} 
                        alt="QR Code WhatsApp" 
                        className="w-48 h-48 object-contain" 
                    />
                </div>
            ) : (
                <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-gray-800 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-dashed border-gray-600 text-gray-500">
                    <FaQrcode className="w-8 h-8" />
                </div>
            )}

            <div className="text-center max-w-xs">
                <h3 className="text-white font-bold mb-1">
                    {connectionStatus?.qrCode ? 'Escaneie o QR Code' : 'WhatsApp Desconectado'}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs">
                    {connectionStatus?.qrCode 
                        ? 'Abra o WhatsApp no seu celular e escaneie o código acima para conectar.' 
                        : 'Integre seu número para enviar mensagens diretamente pelo CRM.'}
                </p>
            </div>

            {!connectionStatus?.qrCode && (
                <button 
                    onClick={handleConnect}
                    className="flex items-center px-6 py-2.5 bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-500 text-white rounded-xl font-bold shadow-lg shadow-green-900/20 transition transform active:scale-95 text-sm"
                >
                    <FaWhatsapp className="mr-2 w-5 h-5" />
                    Gerar QR Code
                </button>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/features/crm/hooks/useCRMIntelligence.ts =====

// src/features/crm/hooks/useCRMIntelligence.ts

import { useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';
import { InteractionAnalysis } from '../types';

/**
 * @hook useCRMIntelligence
 * @description Gerencia a comunicação com os serviços de IA do CRM.
 * @update Agora suporta envio de clientId para análise contextual (RAG).
 */
export function useCRMIntelligence() {
    
    const [isAnalyzing, setIsAnalyzing] = useState(false);
    const [analysisResult, setAnalysisResult] = useState<InteractionAnalysis | null>(null);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    /**
     * @method analyzeText
     * @description Envia um texto bruto para análise de IA no Backend.
     * @param text O conteúdo da interação.
     * @param clientId (Opcional) ID do cliente selecionado para dar contexto à IA.
     */
    const analyzeText = async (text: string, clientId?: string) => {
        setIsAnalyzing(true);
        setError(null);
        setAnalysisResult(null); 

        try {
            // Monta o payload com ou sem contexto
            const payload = { 
                text, 
                clientId: clientId || undefined 
            };

            const response = await api.post('/crm/analyze', payload);
            
            setAnalysisResult(response.data as InteractionAnalysis);
            
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error("Erro na análise de IA:", err);
            const msg = err.response?.data?.message || "Falha ao processar a inteligência da interação.";
            setError(msg);
        } finally {
            setIsAnalyzing(false);
        }
    };

    return {
        analyzeText,
        isAnalyzing,
        analysisResult,
        error,
        clearAnalysis: () => setAnalysisResult(null) 
    };
}


// ===== FILE: src/features/crm/hooks/useClientTimeline.ts =====

// src/features/crm/hooks/useClientTimeline.ts

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';
import { CRMInteraction } from '../types';

/**
 * @hook useClientTimeline
 * @description Gerencia a busca do histórico de interações de um cliente específico.
 * @param clientId ID do cliente selecionado (ou null se nenhum).
 */
export function useClientTimeline(clientId: string | null) {
    
    const [interactions, setInteractions] = useState<CRMInteraction[]>([]);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const fetchTimeline = useCallback(async () => {
        if (!clientId) {
            setInteractions([]);
            return;
        }

        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);

        try {
            // GET na rota que criamos no Backend: /crm/client/:clientId/timeline
            const response = await api.get(`/crm/client/${clientId}/timeline`);
            
            // Tipagem forçada garantida pelo contrato do Backend
            setInteractions(response.data as CRMInteraction[]);
            
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error("Erro ao buscar timeline:", err);
            const msg = err.response?.data?.message || "Falha ao carregar histórico.";
            setError(msg);
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    }, [clientId]);

    // Busca automática sempre que o ID do cliente mudar
    useEffect(() => {
        fetchTimeline();
    }, [fetchTimeline]);

    return {
        interactions,
        isLoading,
        error,
        refreshTimeline: fetchTimeline // Expomos para permitir recarga manual (ex: após enviar msg)
    };
}


// ===== FILE: src/features/crm/hooks/useInteractionMutation.ts =====

// src/features/crm/hooks/useInteractionMutation.ts

import { useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';
import { InteractionAnalysis } from '../types';
// IMPORTAÇÃO CORRETA: Reuso do tipo central
import { CreateClientDto } from '@/core/types/client';

export enum ClientActionType {
    LINK_EXISTING = 'LINK_EXISTING',
    CREATE_NEW = 'CREATE_NEW'
}

// Interface do Payload específico desta rota
export interface RegisterInteractionPayload {
    type: 'NOTE' | 'AUDIO' | 'EMAIL' | 'MEETING';
    content: string;
    aiAnalysis?: InteractionAnalysis;
    action: ClientActionType;
    clientId?: string;       
    // USO CORRETO: Referência ao DTO global
    newClientData?: CreateClientDto; 
}

export function useInteractionMutation() {
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);

    const registerInteraction = async (payload: RegisterInteractionPayload) => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        setSuccess(false);

        try {
            const response = await api.post('/crm/interaction', payload);
            setSuccess(true);
            return response.data;
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error("Erro ao registrar interação:", err);
            const msg = err.response?.data?.message || "Falha ao salvar interação e dados do cliente.";
            setError(msg);
            throw err;
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return {
        registerInteraction,
        isLoading,
        error,
        success,
        resetState: () => {
            setError(null);
            setSuccess(false);
        }
    };
}


// ===== FILE: src/features/crm/types.ts =====

// src/features/crm/types.ts

import { Client } from '@/core/types/client';

export enum DealStatus {
    NEW = 'NEW',
    QUALIFIED = 'QUALIFIED',
    PROPOSAL = 'PROPOSAL',
    NEGOTIATION = 'NEGOTIATION',
    WON = 'WON',
    LOST = 'LOST',
}

export enum InteractionType {
    NOTE = 'NOTE',
    AUDIO = 'AUDIO',
    EMAIL = 'EMAIL',
    MEETING = 'MEETING',
}

// --- NOVO: Interface para o Endereço Extraído pela IA ---
// Porquê: Define a estrutura que esperamos receber do backend (via Zod/LLM).
// Todos os campos são anuláveis (nullable) pois a IA pode não encontrar todos os detalhes no texto.
export interface ExtractedAddress {
    street: string | null;
    number: string | null;
    complement: string | null;
    district: string | null;
    city: string | null;
    state: string | null;
    zipCode: string | null;
    // Tipo literal compatível com o Enum do backend
    type: "MAIN" | "BILLING" | "SHIPPING" | null;
}

/**
 * @interface InteractionAnalysis
 * @description Estrutura exata do JSON retornado pelo endpoint /crm/analyze (Backend).
 * @update Adicionado suporte a lista de endereços extraídos (extractedAddresses).
 */
export interface InteractionAnalysis {
    summary: string;
    sentiment: "POSITIVE" | "NEUTRAL" | "NEGATIVE" | "TENSE";
    tags: string[];
    actionItems: string[];
    dealProbability?: number;
    
    extractedIdentity?: {
        personName: string | null;
        companyName: string | null;
        // Campos granulares de contato
        email: string | null;
        phone: string | null;
        
        // --- NOVO: Lista de Endereços identificados ---
        extractedAddresses?: ExtractedAddress[];

        intentType: "NEW_BUSINESS" | "SUPPORT" | "FOLLOW_UP" | "UNKNOWN";
    };
}

// --- ENTIDADES DO CRM ---

export interface CRMDeal {
    id: string;
    tenantId: string;
    clientId: string;
    title: string;
    status: DealStatus;
    value: number;
    probability: number;
    expectedCloseDate?: string; 
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    
    // Relação (Hidratada se necessário)
    client?: Client;
}

export interface CRMInteraction {
    id: string;
    tenantId: string;
    clientId: string;
    type: InteractionType;
    content: string;
    mediaUrl?: string;
    
    // A Inteligência Anexada
    aiAnalysis?: InteractionAnalysis;
    createdAt: string;
}
